# The Grand Chronology

A legendary epic spanning epochs and lands.

الفصل 1 — طريق الأمل

The winds swept over the quiet valleys as traveler 1 stepped into the morning light. "Every journey of a thousand leagues begins beneath our boots," murmured the elder, tightening the leather strap. The horizon was bathed in gentle dawn gold, whispering secrets of forgotten lore.
